import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react"; import { Pill, Dumbbell, CheckSquare, Droplet, Film, Plus, Check, Trash2, X, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Shuffle, Clock, Flower2, Star, Flame, Pencil, BookOpen, Heart, Wind, CloudRain, Shield, Moon, HelpCircle, BatteryLow, Sun, Gift, Share2, ChevronDown, Youtube, } from "lucide-react"; /* ---------- HELPERS ---------- */ const dateKey = (d) => { const y = d.getFullYear(); const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"); const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0"); return `${y}-${m}-${day}`; }; const todayKey = () => dateKey(new Date()); const dateFromKey = (key) => { const [y, m, d] = key.split("-").map(Number); return new Date(y, m - 1, d); }; const uid = () => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10); const dayOfYear = (d) => { const start = new Date(d.getFullYear(), 0, 0); return Math.floor((d - start) / 86400000); }; const formatTime = (s) => { const m = Math.floor(s / 60); const sec = s % 60; return `${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(sec).padStart(2, "0")}`; }; const HYDRATION_GOAL_ML = 2000; // recommandation moyenne EFSA pour une femme de 18 ans const GLASS_ML = 250; const HYDRATION_GOAL_GLASSES = HYDRATION_GOAL_ML / GLASS_ML; const HYDRATION_MAX_GLASSES = 12; const EMPTY = { tasks: [], pillCycleAnchor: null, pillTaken: {}, hydration: {}, sessionHistory: {}, favoriteSessions: [], favoriteVerses: [], verseReadDates: [], dailyNotes: {}, favoriteFilms: [], wheelOptions: [], }; /* ---------- STORAGE HOOK ---------- */ function useHubData() { const [data, setData] = useState(EMPTY); const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false); const saveTimer = useRef(null); useEffect(() => { (async () => { let initial = null; try { const res = await window.storage.get("hub-data"); if (res && res.value) initial = JSON.parse(res.value); } catch (e) { // pas de données existantes } const CORRECT_ANCHOR = dateKey(new Date(2026, 7, 13)); // plaquette commencée le 13 août 2026 if (!initial) { initial = { ...EMPTY, pillCycleAnchor: CORRECT_ANCHOR, pillTaken: {}, }; } else if (initial.pillCycleAnchor !== CORRECT_ANCHOR) { // corrige une ancienne date d'ancrage stockée précédemment initial = { ...initial, pillCycleAnchor: CORRECT_ANCHOR }; } try { await window.storage.set("hub-data", JSON.stringify(initial)); } catch (e) {} setData({ ...EMPTY, ...initial }); setLoaded(true); })(); }, []); const persist = (next) => { setData(next); if (saveTimer.current) clearTimeout(saveTimer.current); saveTimer.current = setTimeout(async () => { try { await window.storage.set("hub-data", JSON.stringify(next)); } catch (e) { console.error("Erreur de sauvegarde", e); } }, 250); }; return { data, persist, loaded }; } /* ---------- PALETTE ---------- const CREAM = "#FBF3EF"; const CARD = "#FFFFFF"; const TEXT = "#463639"; const TEXT_MUTED = "#A78F92"; const BORDER = "#F0DFE1"; const LOGO_IMG =
